Signal Pad to Emitter connections are traced through metal or resistors <= 300 ohms. For diffusions connected to IO signal PAD through resistors which are marked with layer LUPRESMK are not treated as emitters. Such diffusions don't require double guard rings. This footnote is not checked by DRC. Designers are responsible to use LUPRESMK layer correctly. This marker will open up the connectivity traced from IO signal PAD, LC Power Supply PAD, HC Power Supply PAD, and GROUND PAD.¶

ModifiedVx.C.4Mx and Vx Design Rules--At least 3 (Vx vias spaced <= 0.5 um) must connect Mx to Mx+1 over Weakpoint_REGION, where x = 1-6. Via space to line end within 1 um (run length > 0). There is at least one via spacing to Mx+1_end within 1 um to active the redundancy check.Weakpoint_REGION = (Mx+1 OR Mx+1FILL) density (%) with 100 um tiling within Mx+1EXCLUD < 10%. The region with density less than 10% will be selected to active rule Vx.C.4 check. ,NOTES,
ModifiedVx.EN.Mx.8.RMx and Vx Design Rules>=0.05[Vx touching (Mx with width > 0.295 um)] must be within Mx, where x = 1-6.Some via configurations that fail the "within" check are still permitted to pass this rule. To identify passing vias with an edge failing the "within" check: (a) identify the edge opposite the failing edge, and (b) if there is another via with a run length > 0 um with this opposite edge, the via from step a passes the check.¶
This checking flow permits all normal 2 &timesx n via arrays, where n is >= 2, to pass. In addition, 2 x 1 via arrays will pass, provided that the array spans the metal width.		 ,NOTES,
